That lets these tests pin the exact CLI wording,\n * output handling, exception messages, and log messages each public method\n * produces, without a real pdftract install.\n */\nclass ClientTest extends TestCase\n{\n    private string $tmpDir;\n    private string $binaryPath;\n    private string $argsPath;\n\n    protected function setUp(): void\n    {\n        $this->tmpDir = sys_get_temp_dir() . '/pdftract-php-test-' . bin2hex(random_bytes(6));\n        mkdir($this->tmpDir, 0700);\n        $this->binaryPath = $this->tmpDir . '/pdftract';\n        $this->argsPath = $this->tmpDir . '/argv';\n    }\n\n    protected function tearDown(): void\n    {\n        foreach (glob($this->tmpDir . '/*') ?: [] as $file) {\n            unlink($file);\n        }\n        rmdir($this->tmpDir);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Writes the stand-in binary and returns a client wired to it.\n     */\n    private function client(\n        string $stdout = '',\n        string $stderr = '',\n        int $exitCode = 0,\n        ?AbstractLogger $logger = null\n    ): Client {\n        $script = \"#!/bin/sh\\n\"\n            . \"printf '%s\\\\n' \\\"$@\\\" > \" . escapeshellarg($this->argsPath) . \"\\n\"\n            . \"printf %s '\" . base64_encode($stdout) . \"' | base64 -d\\n\"\n            . \"printf %s '\" . base64_encode($stderr) . \"' | base64 -d >&2\\n\"\n            . \"exit \" . $exitCode . \"\\n\";\n\n        file_put_contents($this->binaryPath, $script);\n        chmod($this->binaryPath, 0700);\n\n        return new Client($this->binaryPath, $logger);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * The argv the stand-in binary was invoked with, one element per argument.\n     *\n     * @return array<int, string>\n     */\n    private function capturedArgs(): array\n    {\n        $raw = file_get_contents($this->argsPath);\n        $this->assertNotFalse($raw, 'binary stand-in did not record its arguments');\n\n        $raw = rtrim($raw, \"\\n\");\n\n        return $raw === '' ? [] : explode(\"\\n\", $raw);\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- extract\n\n    public function testExtractDecodesJsonOutput(): void\n    {\n        $client = $this->client('{\"schema_version\":\"1.0\",\"pages\":[{\"page_index\":0}]}');\n\n        $this->assertSame(\n            ['schema_version' => '1.0', 'pages' => [['page_index' => 0]]],\n            $client->extract('/tmp/doc.pdf')\n        );\n    }\n\n    public function testExtractPassesSourceAndOptionsAsCliArgs(): void\n    {\n        $client = $this->client('{}');\n\n        $client->extract(Source::file('/tmp/doc.pdf'), [\n            'ocrLanguage' => 'eng',\n            'fast' => true,\n            'skipped' => false,\n            'ignored' => null,\n            'maxPages' => 12,\n        ]);\n\n        $this->assertSame(\n            ['/tmp/doc.pdf', '--ocr-language', 'eng', '--fast', '--max-pages', '12'],\n            $this->capturedArgs()\n        );\n    }\n\n    public function testExtractPassesUrlSourceAsUrlFlag(): void\n    {\n        $client = $this->client('{}');\n\n        $client->extract(Source::url('https://example.com/doc.pdf'));\n\n        $this->assertSame(['--url', 'https://example.com/doc.pdf'], $this->capturedArgs());\n    }\n\n    public function testExtractPassesStdinSourceAsDash(): void\n    {\n        $client = $this->client('{}');\n\n        $client->extract(Source::stdin());\n\n        $this->assertSame(['-'], $this->capturedArgs());\n    }\n\n    public function testExtractThrowsOnUndecodableOutput(): void\n    {\n        $client = $this->client('this is not json');\n\n        $this->expectException(PdftractException::class);\n        $this->expectExceptionCode(-1);\n Defaults to 15.0, capped at\n+     *                                        $timeoutSeconds. 0 disables the bound\n+     * @throws ConfigurationException If a timeout is negative or not finite\n      */\n     public function __construct(\n         string $binaryPath = 'pdftract',\n-        ?LoggerInterface $logger = null\n+        ?LoggerInterface $logger = null,\n+        float $timeoutSeconds = @return float Timeout in seconds (0 means unbounded)\n+     */\n+    public function getQuickTimeoutSeconds(): float\n+    {\n+        return $this->quickTimeoutSeconds;\n+    }\n+\n+    /**\n+     * Validate a timeout value\n+     *\n+     * @param float $timeout Timeout in seconds\n+     * @param string $label Name of the value, for error messages\n+     * @return float The validated timeout\n+     * @throws ConfigurationException If the timeout is negative or not finite\n+     */\n+    private function validateTimeout(float $timeout, string $label): float\n+    {\n+        if (!is_finite($timeout) || $timeout < 0) {\n+            throw new ConfigurationException(\n+                sprintf('%s must be a finite, non-negative number of seconds, got: %s', $label, ADR-1's HTTP transport has not landed yet,\n    so this fixes the transport that is actually current: proc_open.\n    \n    Every call now runs through one of two bounded primitives:\n    \n    - run() drives buffered calls (extract, extractText, extractMarkdown,\n      getMetadata, hash, classify, verifyReceipt) under a total wall-clock\n      deadline.\n    - runStream() drives extractStream/search under an *idle* deadline that\n      resets on each chunk, so a long but productive stream is not cut off\n      while a silent stall still is. Abandoning the generator terminates the\n      child instead of leaking it.\n    \n    Both drain stdout and stderr concurrently via stream_select(). That also\n    fixes a second unbounded hang: the old code read stdout to EOF before\n    touching stderr, so a child writing more than a pipe buffer of stderr\n    (~64 KiB) deadlocked the parent outright. There is a regression test for\n    this - against the previous Client it never completes.\n    \n    On expiry the child gets SIGTERM, then SIGKILL after a 2s grace period,\n    and a TimeoutException (extends PdftractException, exit code 124 per GNU\n    timeout(1)) is thrown, so no orphaned pdftract process is left behind.\n    \n    Defaults are 300s for extract/OCR-class calls and 15s for metadata/hash-\n    class calls, both configurable via the constructor and overridable per\n    call with a 'timeout' option; the option is consumed by the client and\n    never forwarded to the CLI. 0 opts out of the bound explicitly.\n    \n    Public method signatures are unchanged - the new constructor parameters\n    are appended and optional.
